We test the generality of these findings in two open-weight models, Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509 and Gemma-4-E4B-it, extracting emotion contrast vectors across all layers, using two model-generated corpora. We recover valence geometry for both models, with peak PC1--valence correlations of $r = 0.76$ and $r = 0.83$, approaching the $r = 0.81$ reported for Claude.Beyond replication, we observe notable differences in how valence representations emerge across model depth. In Gemma-4-E4B-it, valence is strongly encoded in early layers but collapses towards later layers, whereas Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509 exhibits the opposite pattern, with valence representations absent in early layers, but emerging at mid depths. Arousal encoding, in contrast, is sensitive to the extraction corpus: both models show stronger PC2--arousal alignment with Gemma-generated stories ($r$ up to $0.45$) than Apertus-generated ones ($r \leq 0.21$), suggesting arousal-relevant cues are unevenly distributed across generated corpora. We open-source our experiment code and dataset for reproducible investigation of emotion representations across language model architectures.

</details>


### [145] [MinGram: A Minimalist Unigram Tokenizer with High Compression and Competitive Morphological Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27019)
*Sander Land*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MinGram 是一种简化版 Unigram 分词器训练方法，在保留可编辑 token-list 表示的同时，降低训练复杂度，并在压缩率、形态对齐和下游语言模型表现之间取得较好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Unigram tokenizer 的词表表示优雅且易于编辑，但训练过程较重且复杂，包括后缀数组、前向-后向计算和迭代剪枝等步骤。论文希望设计一种更轻量、易实现，同时仍保持高质量分词效果的方法。

Method: 作者提出 MinGram：使用由 BPE 派生的种子词表；采用基于最少 token 路径的 Hard EM；以 token 数为主要优化目标，并仅用 Unigram 分数作为平局时的判定依据；最后使用一次性的平坦分数剪枝，去除标准 Unigram 中复杂的训练组件。

Result: 在六种语言上，MinGram 的压缩效果优于 BPE 和标准 Unigram；其面向压缩的变体可匹配最强 token-count 压缩器，同时保持显著更高的形态对齐。在受控下游语言模型训练中，Unigram 系列分词器整体在 bits-per-byte 指标上稳定优于 BPE，MinGram 表现处于最佳方法之列。

Conclusion: MinGram 表明，Unigram 风格分词器不必依赖复杂训练流程也能获得强性能。通过以最小 token 数为核心、概率分数为辅助，它兼具高压缩率、较好形态一致性和下游建模优势，是一种简洁而有效的 tokenizer 训练方案。

Abstract: The Unigram tokenizer uses an elegant representation which makes it straightforward to edit vocabularies, but its training is comparatively heavy and complex. We introduce MinGram (Minimalist Unigram), which keeps the token-list representation but simplifies training using a BPE-derived seed vocabulary, Hard EM on a minimum-token path, and a single flat score-pruning step. This removes the suffix array, the forward-backward pass, and the iterative prune loop, leaving a procedure that requires little beyond tokenizer inference itself. By making token count the primary objective and using a Unigram score only as a tiebreak, MinGram keeps the compression of pure token-count methods while retaining much of the morphological alignment and downstream quality of probabilistic ones. Across six languages, MinGram compresses better than both BPE and standard Unigram, and a compression-oriented variant matches the strongest token-count compressors while retaining substantially higher morphological alignment. In controlled downstream language-model training, Unigram-family tokenizers, with MinGram among the best, consistently beat BPE in bits-per-byte.

</details>


### [146] [Improving General Role-Playing Agents via Psychology-Grounded Reasoning and Role-Aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27025)
*Zhenhua Xu,Dongsheng Chen,Jian Li,Yitong Lin,Zhebo Wang,Jiafu Wu,Yizhang Jin,Chengjie Wang,Meng Han,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Psy-CoT 与 RAPO，用心理学启发的分步推理和角色感知强化学习优化，提升通用角色扮演智能体的角色一致性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调方法主要学习表层行为模仿，缺乏类似人类的内部思考过程，导致分布外泛化差；同时，基于大模型的奖励模型容易被通用高分短语“奖励黑客”利用，无法区分真正角色相关表达与泛化套话。

Method: Psy-CoT 将回复前推理分解为 Interaction Perception、Psychological Empathy、Logical Construction 三个角色相关步骤，使模型基于角色档案动态思考。RAPO 使用角色档案与生成 token 的互信息来调整策略梯度：在正优势下放大角色特异 token 的梯度，在负优势下减弱其负向更新，从而提升角色相关表达的学习权重。

Result: 在 CoSER、CharacterBench 和 CharacterEval 上，Psy-CoT 优于已有角色扮演 CoT 方法；RAPO 在多个模型规模上稳定超过 GRPO。

Conclusion: 心理学结构化推理与角色感知强化学习的结合，能够缓解表层模仿和奖励黑客问题，更有效地提升角色扮演模型的角色忠实度与泛化能力。

Abstract: Building general-purpose role-playing agents that faithfully portray any character from a natural-language profile remains challenging. The dominant paradigm -- supervised fine-tuning -- encourages behavioral mimicry without deep, human-like internal thought processes, resulting in poor out-of-distribution generalization. Therefore, we propose \textbf{Psy-CoT}, a psychology-grounded chain-of-thought framework that decomposes pre-response reasoning into three role-specific steps -- \emph{Interaction Perception}, \emph{Psychological Empathy}, and \emph{Logical Construction} -- so that the model \emph{thinks dynamically} from the profile rather than merely mimicking surface patterns. While structured reasoning provides a foundation, it alone is insufficient; reinforcement learning is essential to further align the model with character fidelity. However, we observe that under LLM-based reward models, both generic phrases that hack the reward model and genuinely role-specific phrases receive identical gradient signals -- this hacking accumulates over training, misleading the model into treating both as equally optimal choices. To address this, we propose \textbf{Role-Aware Policy Optimization (RAPO)}, which uses profile--token mutual information to weight gradients asymmetrically -- amplifying role-specific tokens under positive advantage while attenuating them under negative advantage. Experiments on CoSER, CharacterBench, and CharacterEval demonstrate that Psy-CoT outperforms existing role-playing CoT methods, and RAPO consistently surpasses GRPO across multiple model scales.

</details>


### [147] [NuclearQAv2: A Structured Benchmark for Evaluating Domain-Science Competence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27047)
*Henry Shaowu Yuchi,Michal Kucer,Benjamin H. Sims,Selma Peterson,Emily Taylor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 NuclearQAv2，一个面向核工程知识的 LLM 评测基准，覆盖布尔、数值和文字问答，用于系统评估模型在技术领域中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在通用任务上表现较强，但在高度技术化领域的可靠性仍难保证；核工程问题不仅需要事实知识，还要求定量推理与概念理解，因此需要专门、系统且可扩展的评测框架。

Method: 作者构建约 1,240 个问答对，分为 boolean、numeric、verbal 三类；数据来源包括专家撰写题目、已有数据集以及基于核工程技术语料的 LLM 辅助生成。框架使用结构化提示进行自动问题生成和回答评估，并在多个 LLM 上开展测试。

Result: 不同模型和不同题型之间表现差异显著。模型通常在事实性问题上表现较好，但在数值推理和概念理解任务上仍面临明显困难。

Conclusion: NuclearQAv2 展示了多维度评测对技术领域 LLM 能力评估的重要性，并可作为可扩展基准，用于衡量模型在核工程等专业领域中的知识、推理和理解能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but ensuring their reliability in highly technical domains remains a significant challenge. In nuclear engineering, problem solving often requires not only factual knowledge but also quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding. To address the need for systematic evaluation in this domain, we introduce NuclearQAv2, a benchmark for assessing LLMs on nuclear engineering knowledge. The benchmark comprises approximately 1,240 question-answer pairs spanning three categories: boolean, numeric, and verbal. NuclearQAv2 is constructed using a hybrid pipeline that combines expert-authored questions, existing datasets, and LLM-assisted generation from domain-specific technical corpora. By leveraging structured prompting for both automated question generation and response evaluation, the proposed framework enables scalable benchmark construction and evaluation. We evaluate a diverse set of LLMs using NuclearQAv2 and observe substantial performance differences across task types. While the models generally perform well on factual questions, quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding remain considerably more challenging. These results highlight the importance of multi-faceted evaluation frameworks and establish NuclearQAv2 as a scalable benchmark for assessing LLM capabilities in technical domains.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Explainable Adjudicative Variance: Quantifying Judicial Discretion via Gated Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27069)
*Stanisław Sójka,Felix Steffek,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向法律结果预测的 Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning 架构，通过区分案件事实与裁判语境，在英国就业法庭数据集上取得优于大规模生成式微调基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律结果预测需要将基于事实证据的实体裁判与可能受裁判者身份、程序性因素影响的技术性处理区分开来；传统提示式或单通道自回归建模难以有效组合法官身份与细粒度结果分类信号。

Method: 引入细粒度结果 taxonomy 监督编码器，作为结构正则化以分离不同语义路径；使用 LoRA 适配的 Gemma-4 编码器，并设计 Gated Fusion 机制动态调节对法官身份嵌入的依赖；与将法官身份和 taxonomy 作为 prompt token 或自回归目标注入的 Gemma-4 26B-A4B SFT 基线比较。

Result: 在 13,937 份英国就业法庭判决上达到新的最佳结果，尤其在最模糊和最稀有的结果类别上提升明显；相比生成式 SFT 基线，所需可训练参数少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 对于身份条件化的法律结果分类，条件信息的结构化、可微组合方式比单纯扩大模型规模或提示式组合更关键；该架构同时提升准确性、参数效率与可解释性。

Abstract: Legal outcome prediction must disentangle objective case facts from adjudicative context. Merit-based rulings rely on factual evidence while technical disposals may hinge on judicial discretion. We propose a Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning architecture that explicitly models this distinction. We introduce a fine-grained outcome taxonomy to supervise the encoder, enforcing a structural regularization that disentangles distinct semantic pathways. This granular legal curriculum enables our Gated Fusion mechanism to dynamically modulate reliance on judge identity. We evaluate our approach on 13,937 UK Employment Tribunal decisions. We benchmark our design against supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a Gemma-4 26B-A4B backbone, in which judge identity and the taxonomy are injected as prompt tokens or autoregressive output targets. The two contextual signals compose only weakly when forced through a single autoregressive channel. In contrast, coupling a LoRA-adapted Gemma-4 encoder with our gated architecture defines a new state of the art on this benchmark while requiring an order of magnitude fewer trainable parameters than the generative SFT baselines, with gains concentrated on the most ambiguous and rarest outcome classes. Beyond accuracy, the architecture is interpretable; learned judge embeddings and calibration profiles localize the cases where adjudicative context drives the prediction. These results indicate that, for identity-conditioned classification of legal outcomes, the choice of conditioning interface dominates scale: differentiable structured composition yields more accurate, more parameter-efficient models than prompt-based composition over a substantially larger backbone.

</details>


### [149] [The Riddle Riddle: Testing Flexible Reasoning in Large Language Models and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27103)
*Bella Fascendini,Kathryn McGregor,Max D. Gupta,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“riddle riddle”范式，用表面上像谜语但只需字面理解的问题检验LLM是否能灵活选择推理策略。结果显示，LLM擅长真实谜语却在“伪谜语”上显著下降，而人类呈相反模式，说明LLM的高表现可能更多来自记忆检索或表面模式匹配，而非真正的灵活推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是区分大型语言模型在认知任务中的高准确率究竟源于训练数据中的模式匹配/记忆检索，还是源于像人类一样根据问题内容灵活调整推理策略。传统谜语任务可能难以区分这两者，因为模型可能记住常见谜语答案。

Method: 作者设计了“riddle riddle paradigm”：将问题写成类似流行谜语的形式，但答案只需要字面解释。若模型依赖表面形式，会错误地采用创造性/谜语式推理；若模型基于内容推理，则应切换到字面策略。研究包含两个实验，比较九个先进LLM与100名人类参与者在真实谜语和“伪谜语”上的表现，并进行错误类型分析。

Result: LLM在真实谜语上的准确率远高于“伪谜语”（84.9% vs. 50.7%）；人类则相反，在“伪谜语”上高于真实谜语（80.5% vs. 50.5%）。错误分析显示，LLM在“伪谜语”中的错误有90.8%源于不恰当地使用创造性推理；人类在真实谜语中的错误有57.6%源于过度使用字面推理。

Conclusion: 论文认为，LLM在真实谜语上的强表现不一定代表灵活推理能力，可能反映了对常见谜语答案的记忆检索或对表面结构的模式响应。若缺少专门设计来区分策略选择与表面拟合的刺激材料，人们容易将看似推理的LLM输出误判为真正推理。

Abstract: Humans flexibly adapt their reasoning strategies to the requirements of a given problem. Large language models (LLMs) have performed well on many cognitive tasks, however, it is unclear whether this accuracy is a result of pattern matching from training data or flexible reasoning. Here, we introduce a novel paradigm to test this question: the riddle riddle paradigm. Riddle riddles are word problems written to mimic popular riddles, but altered so their answers only require literal interpretations. Identifying correct answers requires looking past the structure of each question and flexibly apply different reasoning strategies based on the content. If LLMs respond to surface features, such as form, a riddle-like structure should cause models to use an inventive reasoning strategy even when a literal interpretation suffices. Alternatively, if LLMs reason based on content, they should flexibly switch strategies when appropriate. Across two experiments with nine state-of-the-art LLMs and 100 human participants, we show humans and LLMs fail on this paradigm in opposite directions. LLMs were far more accurate on genuine riddles than on riddle riddles (84.9% vs. 50.7%); whereas humans showed the reverse effect (50.5% vs. 80.5%). Error analysis shows that 90.8% of LLM errors on riddle riddles (the condition where they show diminished performance) were due to inappropriate use of inventive reasoning while only 57.6% of human errors on genuine riddles were due to overextending literal reasoning. Thus, while both groups make mistakes, reasoning mistakes are made more often by LLMs than by humans. Overall, LLMs' strong performance on genuine riddles may reflect memory retrieval rather than flexible strategy selection, and without stimuli designed to elicit this contrast, it becomes easy to conflate LLM-generated outputs that look like reasoning with genuine reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [Forecasting With LLMs: Improved Generalization Through Feature Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27199)
*Humzah Merchant,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过解释性时间特征干预，降低大语言模型在预测任务中的“前视偏差”，并提升其基于历史信息进行推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功预测需要模型识别历史状态与未来状态之间可泛化的模式，而不是依赖特定时间点的知识或隐含的未来信息。作者希望理解LLM在预测时是否使用时间感知推理，或是否存在看似提前知道未来的偏差。

Method: 作者将LLM应用于多种预测任务，并使用稀疏自编码器分析模型内部状态，识别与时间感知推理和前视偏差相关的特征。随后在另一个不同领域的任务中，对这些特征进行干预，测试其因果作用。

Result: 分析发现了与时间感知推理和候选前视偏差推理相关的内部特征。放大时间感知特征能够显著降低预测提示中的前视偏差，同时保持模型的一般推理能力；而操控候选前视偏差特征没有明显效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LLM内部存在可解释的时间相关特征，对这些特征进行因果干预可以使模型更倾向于基于历史信息进行稳健预测，而非依赖前视偏差。

Abstract: Successful forecasting involves identifying patterns between historical and future states of the world which generalize to future observations. We apply LLMs to a variety of forecasting tasks and inspect their internal states using sparse autoencoders to understand whether they appear to rely on time-specific pieces of knowledge versus generalizable patterns. Our analyses identify features associated with both time-aware reasoning and look-ahead-biased reasoning. We then apply the LLMs to an entirely different domain and intervene on these features. We find that amplifying time-awareness features substantially reduces look-ahead bias on forecasting prompts while preserving general reasoning performance. In contrast, steering the candidate look-ahead-bias features does not produce an effect. These results suggest that interpretable temporal features can be used to causally shift LLMs toward more historically grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [151] [Syntactic Belief Update as the Driver of Garden Path Processing Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27206)
*Alan Zhou,Miloš Stanojević,John T. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用“句法信念更新”替代/补充词汇惊讶度来解释花园径句的人类阅读困难，并显示其与阅读时数据拟合更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 花园径句中，句子前缀会诱导读者形成错误句法解释，关键词出现后需要重新分析；传统词汇惊讶度虽常能预测加工难度，但在此类句子上预测不足，因此需要一种不依赖词项概率、能刻画句法重分析成本的指标。

Method: 作者构建一种逐词预测句法树概率分布的处理器，将该分布称为句法信念；每读入一个新词后更新分布，并用广义 Rényi 散度衡量更新幅度。该指标依赖具体词项引发的句法证据，但独立于词项本身出现概率。

Result: 在花园径句的人类阅读时数据上，句法信念更新相比词汇惊讶度提供了更好的拟合，尤其能捕捉关键位置因错误句法预期被推翻而产生的加工困难。

Conclusion: 花园径句的理解困难更适合被解释为句法信念的大幅更新，而不只是低概率词项导致的惊讶；该研究提示心理语言学可进一步探索非词汇概率驱动的、纯句法层面的 surprisal 替代指标。

Abstract: Garden path sentences present a processing difficulty for humans -- the sentence prefix leads the listener towards one interpretation, until the listener hears a critical word that shows that the initial interpretation was wrong. Lexical surprisal, a measure that usually predicts sentence processing difficulty quite well, fails to provide good predictions for garden path sentences.
  We propose an alternative that actively predicts a probability distribution over syntactic trees (its syntactic belief) and updates that distribution after each new word. If a processor is led down a garden path, syntactic beliefs will be wrong and will require a large update at the critical word. The magnitude of the update is measured with a generalized Rényi divergence. Crucially, this metric is dependent on lexical items, but is fully independent of the probability of lexical items. This Syntactic Belief Update provides a better fit to the human reading time data on garden path sentences. This suggests a new research direction examining purely non-lexical alternatives to surprisal for psycholinguistics.

</details>


### [152] [Paved with True Intents: Intent-Aware Training Improves LLM Safety Classification Across Training Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27210)
*Jeremias Ferrao,Niclas Müller-Hof,Iustin Sîrbu,Traian Rebedea,Yftah Ziser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出将“用户意图”作为提示与安全标签之间的显式中间信号，并构建 AIMS 数据集验证其对安全分类器训练的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器通常直接从用户提示预测最终安全/危害标签，容易忽略提示背后的真实意图，尤其在困难或边界案例中表现不稳。作者希望证明显式建模用户意图能提供更紧凑、更高质量的监督信号，从而提升鲁棒性。

Method: 作者构建了 AIMS：包含 1,724 条困难安全提示，每条都有人类标注的意图描述和危害标签。基于该数据集，比较多种意图感知训练范式，包括监督微调（SFT）、偏好学习（DPO）、推理蒸馏、意图条件蒸馏，以及使用 GRPO 对意图忠实性进行直接奖励的强化学习方法。

Result: 尽管 AIMS 规模较小，但能训练出有竞争力的安全分类器。基于模型生成意图错误的 DPO 优于 SFT；意图条件蒸馏在多数教师-学生组合中优于仅推理蒸馏；直接奖励意图忠实性的 GRPO 在五个外部安全基准上取得最强平均表现。同时，意图感知模型构成了推理延迟与 F1 性能之间的 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: 显式且忠实的用户意图建模是一种高效的监督信号，可在有限标注数据下提升安全分类器的泛化能力、鲁棒性和效率表现。

Abstract: We argue that safety classifiers should model user intent as an explicit signal between the prompt and the final label. To study this, we introduce AIMS, a human-annotated dataset of 1,724 difficult safety prompts, each paired with an intent description and harm label. We use AIMS to evaluate intent-aware training across supervised fine-tuning, preference learning, reasoning distillation, and reinforcement learning. Despite its size, AIMS enables competitive safety classifiers across training regimes: DPO from model-generated intent errors improves over SFT, and intent-conditioned distillation outperforms reasoning-only distillation in most teacher-student pairs. Most notably, directly rewarding intent faithfulness with GRPO yields the strongest average performance across five external safety benchmarks, while our intent-aware models form the inference latency-F1 Pareto frontier. These results show that faithful intent modeling is a compact, high-quality supervision signal for more robust safety classifiers.

</details>


### [153] [Compositionality and the lexicon in evolutionary semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27228)
*Fausto Carcassi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一个将形式语义学的递归组合思想纳入演化建模的框架，用于解释量化意义中的语义普遍性，尤其是保守性为何会作为高效的系统级抽象涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有语义普遍性研究通常要么把词汇作为固定信号结构来建模，要么采用整体式组合而缺乏可解释的词汇部分，未能充分体现形式语义学中“句义由词义递归组合而成”的核心洞见。作者希望弥合形式语义学与演化模型之间的缺口。

Method: 作者构建了一个允许词汇意义与组合函数共同演化的模型，并在概念简单性与交际准确性两种压力下分析其演化结果。研究对象是量化意义，并通过帕累托前沿考察不同系统在效率、简单性和准确性之间的权衡。

Result: 分析显示，保守性这一著名语义普遍性可以作为一种高效的系统级抽象自然涌现。该模型对句法结构敏感，并能解释量词可学习性证据与先前演化模型之间存在的张力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，形式语义学关于句子意义组合生成的理论图景可以有效结合演化建模。所提出的框架可作为研究语义普遍性的通用模板，尤其适用于涉及语法类别内部全局压缩、句法论元语义专门化以及词汇意义和组合意义共同演化的现象。

Abstract: Formal semantics has shown that sentence meanings arise by recursively composing lexical meanings, yet much of the literature on semantic universals models either lexicons with fixed signal structures or holistic composition without interpretable lexical parts. We introduce a framework that integrates this fundamental insight of formal semantics in evolutionary modeling, by allowing lexical meanings and a composition function to co-evolve under pressures for conceptual simplicity and communicative accuracy. We apply this framework to the evolution of quantificational meaning. Analyzing the Pareto frontier, we find that the most well-known semantic universal, conservativity, emerges as an efficient system-wide abstraction. The account is sensitive to syntactic structure and helps reconcile tensions between empirical evidence on quantifier learnability and prior evolutionary models. More broadly, the results demonstrate that the picture of sentential meaning developed in formal semantics can be productively combined with evolutionary modeling. The framework offers a template for studying universals that involve global compression within a grammatical category, semantic specialization of syntactic arguments, and the co-evolution of lexical and compositional meaning.

</details>


### [154] [CARVE: Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency for Chunk-Parallel Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27229)
*Sayak Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CARVE：一种内容感知、参数更高效的循环架构，通过仅在 key 轴执行 erase，使高效 WY-form 分块求解器保持有效，同时改善门控机制、参数量和显存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GDN-2 架构存在三点耦合缺陷：erase gate 不感知当前记忆内容；value 轴 erase mask 参数开销大；value 轴 erase 在数学上阻碍 WY-form triangular chunk solver，从而影响循环模型训练效率。

Method: CARVE 将 erase 操作限制在 key 轴，并证明这是 WY-form solver 有效的充要条件；复用 recurrent output tensor 作为 erase gate 的内容信号；将逐 value 的 write-gate 投影替换为每个 head 一个标量。初始化时与 GDN-2 位级一致。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数、100B tokens 训练设置下，CARVE 的 WikiText perplexity 为 15.72，比 GDN-2 低 0.18；在九个常识推理基准中领先所有循环基线；在所有 RULER retrieval probes 上达到 SOTA；吞吐开销仅 0.4%，峰值显存降低 13%，参数减少 19%。

Conclusion: 摘要声称 CARVE 以 key-axis erase 为核心统一解决内容盲门控、参数低效和训练求解器不兼容问题，在保持高效训练的同时提升性能与资源效率，并给出多项理论支撑。

Abstract: Recurrent models must forget in order to remember, yet the state of the art decides what to erase without consulting what is stored -- the gate sees only the arriving token, not the memory it is about to modify. This memory-blind gating is one of three coupled defects in the leading delta-rule architecture (GDN-2): the value-axis erase mask wastes parameters at the scale of the value projection, and -- as we prove -- mathematically prevents the WY-form triangular chunk solver that makes recurrent training competitive with Transformers.
  We introduce CARVE (Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency), which resolves all three problems through one principle: erase only on the key axis. This is provably necessary and sufficient for the WY-form solver to remain valid. Within it, CARVE reuses the recurrent output tensor -- already written to GPU memory -- as a free content signal for the erase gate, and replaces the per-value write-gate projection with a single scalar per head. At initialisation CARVE is bit-identical to GDN-2; any quality difference emerges from what the content gate learns.
  At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B tokens, CARVE achieves WikiText perplexity 15.72 (minus 0.18 vs. GDN-2, a 4.5-sigma effect), leads every recurrent baseline on nine common-sense reasoning benchmarks, and sets state of the art on every RULER retrieval probe -- at 0.4% throughput overhead, 13% lower peak memory, and 19% fewer parameters. Six formal theorems cover memory capacity, Lyapunov stability, gradient flow, expressivity separation, Pareto-optimal chunk size, and hybrid optimality.

</details>


### [155] [Bridging Talk and Thought: Understanding Dialogue Dynamics Across Collaborative Problem-Solving Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27233)
*Zhengyuan Liu,Stella Xin Yin,Min-Yen Kan,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于分析协作问题解决场景中对话的概念框架，尤其关注人类-AI与多智能体协作中的互动动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能系统具备自主推理与策略性合作能力，研究协作问题解决过程中的对话互动对于优化和评估人机/多智能体合作变得重要；现有分析方法在刻画认知、非认知与调节机制方面存在不足。

Method: 提出分层的双层编码方案，将认知与非认知问题解决行为同元认知调节机制整合起来，并在九个跨领域数据集上验证其有效性与泛化性。

Result: 框架能够揭示人类与智能体如何协调知识、技能和努力以解决复杂问题，并显示元认知调节可作为识别更深层次协作的重要区分因素。

Conclusion: 该研究为协作问题解决对话分析提供了更系统、可推广的框架，强调元认知调节在人机和多智能体深度协作评估中的关键作用。

Abstract: We present a conceptual framework for analyzing dialogue in collaborative problem-solving contexts, with an emphasis on the emerging dynamics of human-AI and multi-agent collaboration. As intelligent systems become active agents capable of autonomous reasoning and strategic cooperation, understanding the dialogic interaction during collaborative problem solving is increasingly important for optimizing and evaluating such partnerships. Our framework addresses key limitations in current analytical approaches through a hierarchical two-layer coding scheme that integrates cognitive and non-cognitive problem solving with metacognitive regulatory mechanisms. We demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability across nine datasets spanning multiple domains, and provide insights into how humans and agents coordinate their knowledge, skills, and efforts to solve complex problems, showing in particular that metacognitive regulation can be an essential discriminator of deeper collaboration.

</details>


### [156] [LMs as Task-Specific Knowledge Bases: An Interpretability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27237)
*Amit Elhelo,Amir Globerson,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型是否像知识库一样能对同一事实在不同查询形式下给出一致答案，结论是否定的：知识往往以任务特定方式编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型包含大量事实知识，因此常被类比为参数化知识库。但真正的知识库应具备“单一事实源”属性，即同一事实在不同查询方式下应保持一致。作者希望检验语言模型是否满足这一性质。

Method: 论文从行为层面与机制层面分析语言模型。行为分析考察训练过程中某一任务上习得的事实是否会在其他任务中同步出现；机制分析通过参数定位实验识别同一事实在不同任务中依赖的参数子集；同时分析链式思维推理为何能提升效果。

Result: 实验发现，同一事实在一种任务中被模型掌握后，常常不会在其他任务中同时表现出来。参数定位显示，不同任务即使涉及同一事实，也可能依赖不同的参数子集。链式思维的有效性部分来自于激活了评估任务之外的任务特定参数。

Conclusion: 语言模型中的事实知识并非像传统知识库那样由统一来源一致调用，而是与提问方式和任务形式深度绑定。这削弱了“语言模型参数即知识库”的类比，并对事实知识的可靠性、可控性和一致性提出挑战。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) capture large amounts of factual knowledge applicable to a wide range of tasks, motivating the view of their parameters as a knowledge base. An important property of knowledge bases is that different queries for the same fact return consistent results, drawing on a single source of truth. We investigate whether LMs satisfy this property through behavioral and mechanistic analyses. Our results suggest that they encode knowledge in a task-specific manner. Behaviorally, facts acquired on one task frequently fail to co-emerge on others during training. Parameter localization experiments suggest a mechanistic explanation, revealing distinct parameter subsets underlying different tasks for the same fact. Finally, we show that chain-of-thought reasoning draws part of its effectiveness from engaging task-specific parameters beyond those tied to the evaluation task. Our findings suggest that what the model knows and how it is asked are intertwined in parameter space, undermining the "knowledge base" analogy and carrying implications for the reliability and controllability of factual knowledge in LMs.

</details>


### [157] [How Surprising Is Historical Italian to Language Models? Tokenization Tax, Comprehension Tax, and a Simple Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27275)
*Maria Levchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，将大语言模型处理历史文本的困难拆分为分词成本、预测不确定性、语义鲁棒性和上下文敏感性四个维度；结果表明历史文本会带来稳定编码成本，但语义表示仍较稳健，简单时间上下文提示可显著降低困惑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字图书馆工作流越来越依赖大语言模型，但模型处理历史语言的能力尚不清楚。既有研究常将历史文本难度视为单一障碍，混淆了正字法差异、语言距离和预训练暴露程度等因素，因此需要更细粒度的诊断方法。

Method: 作者构建四维诊断框架：tokenization cost、surprisal、semantic robustness、context sensitivity；并在三个跨世纪数据集上评估，包括新整理的17世纪意大利语语料、作为高暴露对照的19世纪意大利语经典文本《I Promessi Sposi》，以及18世纪俄语民用印刷书籍作为正字法压力测试。

Result: 俄语与早期现代意大利语均产生约25–30%的分词膨胀，但预测难度显著不同：17世纪意大利语平均surprisal为现代等价文本的2.4倍，学术散文达3.2倍，而俄语仅小幅增加。与此同时，各数据集嵌入相似度均保持在0.85以上，说明预测不稳定不等于语义表示退化。加入最小时间上下文提示可将历史文本surprisal降低约60%。

Conclusion: 历史文本对LLM施加一致的编码税，但模型仍能较好保持语义表示。因此，数字图书馆可较安全地将LLM用于语义检索等任务；但生成式应用需要通过上下文提示等方式进行适配，以降低历史语言带来的预测不确定性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly critical to digital library workflows, yet their ability to process historical language remains poorly understood. Historical difficulty is typically treated as a monolithic barrier, conflating orthographic variation, linguistic distance, and pretraining exposure. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic framework that decomposes this difficulty into four distinct dimensions: tokenization cost, predictive uncertainty (surprisal), semantic robustness, and context sensitivity.
  We evaluate this framework on three datasets spanning three centuries: (1) a newly curated corpus of 17th-century Italian texts (1610-1689) digitized from original page images; (2) canonical 19th-century Italian "I Promessi Sposi" serving as a high-exposure control; and (3) 18th-century Russian civil print books as a contrastive orthographic stress test.
  Our results reveal a distinct dissociation between encoding cost and comprehension. While Russian and early modern Italian incur comparable tokenization penalties (25-30% inflation), their predictive difficulty diverges sharply. 17th-century Italian is on average 2.4 times more surprising than its modern equivalent - with academic prose reaching 3.2 times - whereas Russian shows only a modest increase. But predictive uncertainty does not imply representational degradation: embedding similarity remains robust (> 0.85) across all datasets, confirming that models can represent historical meaning even when generation is unstable.
  Finally, we demonstrate that a minimal temporal context prompt reduces historical surprisal by approximately 60%, offering a simple, model-agnostic mitigation. These findings suggest that while historical text imposes a consistent encoding tax, digital libraries can safely deploy LLMs for semantic retrieval tasks, provided that generative applications are carefully adapted.

</details>


### [158] [Multilingual Reasoning Cascades Need More Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27306)
*Arnav Mazumder,Dengjia Zhang,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov,Niyati Bafna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究一种无需训练的上下文感知翻译级联方法，在最终翻译答案时保留原始问题、英文译文与推理轨迹，以提升多语言推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统翻译级联先将非英语问题译为英语、用英语推理，再把答案译回原语言；该流程具有竞争力但存在结构性信息损失，可能丢失文化语境、语体、歧义消解等对最终回答有用的线索，并导致误差传播。

Method: 作者提出 context-aware translation cascade：在最终翻译模块的上下文中额外提供原始语言问题、英文翻译问题以及推理过程。该方法不需要额外训练。实验覆盖九个多语言基准、多种任务类型、三个骨干模型，以及285种高、中、低资源语言，并分析不同上下文来源的贡献。

Result: 该方法在开放式生成任务上跨模型、跨语言资源水平均带来显著提升。消融分析表明，原始语言问题是最主要的有益上下文来源。

Conclusion: 多语言推理中的翻译级联应更重视信息流设计，以缓解级联误差传播。一个简单可行的默认策略是：在整个流水线中持续保留原始用户问题，直到最终答案翻译阶段。

Abstract: Translation cascades for reasoning translate the query from another language to English, reason in English, and translate the answer back to the original language. This is a competitive approach to multilingual reasoning, but structurally lossy, since each stage discards information later stages may need, including cues for cultural grounding, register, and disambiguation. We examine the benefits of a simple and training-free intervention: a context-aware translation cascade, which additionally provides the original question, the English translated question, and the reasoning trace to the context of the final translation module. We evaluate gains across nine multilingual benchmarks including various task types, three backbone models, and 285 high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, and demonstrate strong gains for open-ended generation across models and resource regimes. We show that the original language question carries most of the beneficial context. Our study emphasizes the need to better design information flow in machine translation cascades for mitigating error propagation, and provides a simple and actionable default strategy: preserve the original user question until the end of the pipeline.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Surface Forms: A Comprehensive, Mechanism-Oriented Taxonomy of Indirect Linguistic Encoding for LLM-Based Coded Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27314)
*Hamid Reza Firoozfar,Mohammadsadegh Abolhasani,Reza Mousavi,Paul Jen-Hwa Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向编码机制的间接语言表达（ILE）分类体系，并证明其能提升大模型对新兴隐晦/编码语言的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户会使用算法黑话、委婉语或对抗性混淆等间接表达来规避审核与监测。既有分类多按交际意图或场景划分，难以稳定覆盖不断演化的编码语言，因此需要一种更通用、机制导向的分类框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个综合性的、机制导向的ILE分类法，关注意义被编码与恢复时所依赖的操作机制，而非表达目的。随后将该分类法融入LLM提示词，并与四种既有分类法及无分类基线进行比较；实验数据为2,000条人工标注的TikTok与Bluesky帖子，在三个LLM上评估文档级与片段级表现。

Result: 所提分类法在三个LLM上的文档级和片段级任务中均取得最佳表现；相较最强基准，准确率提升4.7%，F1提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 综合且机制导向的分类体系可作为检测新兴编码语言的稳定支架，并能为内容审核任务提供有效输入。

Abstract: To avoid moderation and surveillance on social media, some users routinely invent indirect linguistic expressions (ILE) that camouflage sensitive meanings. Such expressions surface as algospeak, euphemisms, and adversarial obfuscation, depending on intent and context, and they involve recurring encoding mechanisms. We propose a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy of ILE that abstracts away from communicative goals and instead categorizes the underlying operations through which meaning is encoded and recovered. We evaluate the taxonomy by incorporating it into LLM prompts and comparing it with four existing taxonomies and a no-taxonomy baseline, using 2,000 manually annotated TikTok and Bluesky posts. The proposed taxonomy attains the strongest document- and span-level performance across the three LLMs, achieving an improvement of 4.7% in accuracy and 5.4% in F1 over the best-performing benchmark. The empirical results reveal the importance of a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy as a stable scaffold for detecting emerging coded language and a useful input to content moderation. Disclaimer: This paper contains content that may be profane, vulgar, or offensive.

</details>


### [160] [LLM-Based Examination of Eligibility Criteria from Securities Prospectuses at the German Central Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27316)
*Serhii Hamotskyi,Akash Kumar Gautam,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将大语言模型用于证券抵押品资格审核中的信息抽取与判定，替代或补充传统NER方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国中央银行需要从冗长、半结构化且常含德英双语内容的招股说明书中核验证券是否符合抵押品资格；人工审核成本高，传统NER又易受OCR噪声、语言差异、跨度约束和标注数据需求限制。

Method: 提出生成式信息抽取流程，将任务分解为抽取、规范化和解释三个阶段；利用LLM处理噪声文本和德英混合内容，并引入基于值的LLM-as-a-judge评估方法，以语义层面替代单纯位置匹配评估。

Result: LLM系统在文档级资格判定上达到较高精度，最高可达91%，并表现出保守的运行特征，能够减少误接受。

Conclusion: LLM可有效支持复杂金融法律文档中的抵押品资格审核，生成式信息抽取比传统NER更灵活，尤其适合噪声、多语言和半结构化场景。

Abstract: Verifying the eligibility of securities as collateral is a key responsibility of the German Central Bank. However, manually verifying these assets against legal and financial criteria within lengthy, semi-structured, and often bilingual prospectuses is a resource-intensive task. While previous efforts utilized traditional Named Entity Recognition (NER) for information extraction, these methods can struggle with OCR noise, linguistic variance, and rigid span-based constraints, and the need for manually annotated training data for each relevant annotation type. In this paper, we present the first case study applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to the eligibility examination process, shifting the paradigm toward a generative Information Extraction pipeline. Our approach decomposes the task into extraction, normalization, and interpretation, allowing for greater flexibility in handling noisy text and interleaved German-English content. We further introduce a value-based evaluation methodology using LLM-as-a-judge, which offers a more semantic assessment than location-based metrics. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based systems achieve high precision (up to 91%) in document-level eligibility, exhibiting a conservative operating profile that minimizes false acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27330)
*Tianyi Men,Zhuoran Jin,Pengfei Cao,Yubo Chen,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出PEEU方法，通过自主探索网页环境获取经验，并利用事后经验合成严格对齐的高层任务规划训练数据，以提升小型开源多模态大模型在GUI任务中的规划与跨网站泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开源MLLM具备低成本和隐私优势，但在多模态网页代理场景中存在任务规划能力弱、跨网站泛化能力有限的问题；复杂GUI任务需要被有效分解为可执行动作，因此亟需提升小模型的高层规划与OOD泛化能力。

Method: 作者提出Planning Experience Exploration and Utilization（PEEU）：让智能体自主探索环境以发现可复用经验，并利用hindsight experience生成与任务严格对齐的高层训练数据。同时提出Task Decomposition Hierarchical Analysis Framework（TDHAF），从低层、中层、高层三个任务粒度定量分析组合泛化行为。

Result: 实验表明PEEU在真实世界基准上效果显著，训练后的7B模型达到30.6%准确率，并超过更大的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型。分析发现，掌握低层原子技能并不能保证高层规划能力，而高层任务训练能带来更强的OOD泛化。

Conclusion: 构建基于事后经验的高层任务数据，并有效利用探索经验，是提升小型MLLM在多模态网页代理中OOD规划能力的关键。

Abstract: Multimodal web agents can assist humans in operating repetitive GUI tasks, where effective task planning is essential for decomposing complex tasks into executable actions. While small open source MLLMs are cost efficient and privacy preserving compared with commercial large models, they suffer from weak planning and limited cross website generalization. To address these limitations, we introduce the planning experience exploration and utilization (PEEU) method, which autonomously explores environments to discover experiences and utilizes hindsight experience to synthesize strictly aligned, high level training data. To quantitatively analyze the generalization behaviors driving this performance, we propose the task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework (TDHAF) to systematically study compositional generalization across three task granularities: low, middle and high levels. Our analysis reveals that mastering low level atomic skills does not guarantee high level planning competence, while high level task training yields stronger OOD generalization. Experiments on real world benchmarks demonstrate PEEU's superior effectiveness: our 7B model achieves 30.6% accuracy, outperforming the much larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model. These demonstrate constructing hindsight high level tasks and leveraging experiences is crucial for OOD planning abilities of small MLLMs.

</details>


### [162] [Mapping Political-Elite Networks in Europe with a Multilingual Joint Entity-Relation Extraction Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27347)
*Kirill Solovev,Jana Lasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Whether political elites organise into rent-seeking coalitions that capture public resources or civic networks that sustain governance is a central question in comparative politics. Yet observing these complex, informal, and adversarial ties at scale has historically required intensive manual coding, while automated text-as-data methods have largely been limited to simple co-occurrence. Recent large language model (LLM) approaches offer a path forward but often rely on proprietary APIs, lack cross-lingual capability, and struggle with scalable entity resolution. We present a modular, fully open-weight pipeline for multilingual joint entity-relation extraction that builds signed, temporal knowledge graphs from massive unstructured news corpora. It combines span-based named-entity recognition (NER) with a three-stage linking cascade mapping mentions to language-independent Wikidata identifiers; a high-throughput, ontology-constrained mixture-of-experts model then uses guided decoding to extract directed, signed relationships grounded in a domain ontology. A full-coverage spot-check against a 3491-relation gold standard shows high textual correctness (68.2% strict to 93.7% lenient). Two large-scale case studies validate the pipeline against the public record. In Austria, it reconstructs a political party's complete lifecycle, dating internal fractures and tracking personnel into successor factions and court convictions. In a Polish corpus, it uncovers the overlapping economic and governance networks of state-enterprise patronage, alongside the structurally balanced, signed conflict network of the polarized Civic Platform (Platforma Obywatelska, PO)--Law and Justice (Prawo i Sprawiedliwość, PiS) duopoly. By bridging raw multilingual text and structured relational data, our framework provides a robust, replicable foundation for cross-national empirical computational social science.

</details>

### [163] [Deletion-Correcting Codes for the $\ell$-Symbol Read Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26434)
*Zuo Ye,Gennian Ge*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究ℓ-symbol read channel中的删除纠错码，刻画读向量中ℓ-mer删除后的结构效应，并基于该结构构造低冗余的删除纠错码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该信道模型源于纳米孔测序、带连续读头的赛道存储器以及相关序列标记问题，其中接收端观测的是发送序列所有连续ℓ-mer组成的读向量；实际系统中可能发生若干ℓ-mer被删除，因此需要设计能在对抗性删除下恢复信息的纠错码。

Method: 作者首先给出删除ℓ-mer后恢复一致性所需插入操作的结构刻画，证明恢复后的序列等价于原序列在特定周期子串中删除符号；当t≤ℓ−2时，这些删除对应完整最小周期。随后引入check patterns，并利用power-sum syndromes构造ℓ-read删除纠错码，同时针对若干特殊参数对设计改进构造。

Result: 对任意ℓ≥2，得到单删除纠错码，冗余为log⌊(n+2ℓ)/(ℓ−1)⌋。当2≤t≤ℓ/2时，构造q元ℓ-read t删除纠错码，冗余为tlog n+O(1)。当ℓ=2t−1且t≥3时，构造冗余为(2t−1)log n+O(1)的码。对(ℓ,t)∈{(2,2),(3,2),(3,3)}给出更优结果，包括二元ℓ-read 2删除码冗余2log n+O(1)，非二元3-read 2删除码冗余3log n+O(1)，二元3-read 3删除码冗余5log n+O(1)，非二元3-read 3删除码冗余7log n+O(1)。

Conclusion: 该工作将ℓ-mer读向量删除问题转化为具有周期结构的符号删除问题，并据此系统性地构造了多类低冗余删除纠错码，扩展了删除纠错码在重叠读信道中的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies deletion-correcting codes for the $\ell$-symbol read channel, whose noiseless output is the vector of all consecutive $\ell$-mers of a transmitted sequence. This model is motivated by overlapping-read mechanisms arising in nanopore sequencing, racetrack memories with consecutive read heads, and related sequence-labeling problems. We consider an adversarial setting in which a fixed number of $\ell$-mers are deleted from the read vector. Our first contribution is a structural characterization of the effect of such deletions: after a minimum number of $\ell$-mers are inserted to restore consistency, the resulting sequence is obtained from the transmitted sequence by deleting symbols from certain periodic substrings; when $t\le \ell-2$, these deletions correspond to complete minimum periods. Based on this characterization, we introduce check patterns and construct $\ell$-read deletion-correcting codes via power-sum syndromes. For every $\ell\ge2$, we obtain single-deletion correcting codes with redundancy $\log\lfloor (n+2\ell)/(\ell-1)\rfloor$. For $2\le t\le \ell/2$, we construct $q$-ary $\ell$-read $t$-deletion correcting codes with redundancy $t\log n+O(1)$, and for $\ell=2t-1$ with $t\ge3$, we construct codes with redundancy $(2t-1)\log n+O(1)$. We also study the sporadic parameter pairs $(\ell,t)\in\{(2,2),(3,2),(3,3)\}$ and obtain improved constructions, including binary $\ell$-read $2$-deletion correcting codes with redundancy $2\log n+O(1)$ for $\ell=2,3$, a non-binary $3$-read $2$-deletion correcting code with redundancy $3\log n+O(1)$, a binary $3$-read $3$-deletion correcting code with redundancy $5\log n+O(1)$, and a non-binary $3$-read $3$-deletion correcting code with redundancy $7\log n+O(1)$.

</details>


### [164] [A Low-PAPR, Synchronization-Robust Non-Coherent Grassmannian Modulation for Optical Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26464)
*Eylon E. Krause*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向非相干Grassmannian（酉空时）信号的低PAPR常模星座设计与相位盲子空间定时误差检测器，在无需载波相位/偏振恢复和导频的条件下实现低峰均比、定时恢复与分集增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Grassmannian星座研究多假设线性无失真信道/发射机且符号定时已获得，未充分考虑实际系统中的发射机非线性、光调制器线性范围、Kerr非线性惩罚以及非相干接收下的时钟恢复问题。

Method: 作者在现有Grassmannian packing基础上加入常模约束，评估PAPR与弦距之间的折中；同时基于GLRT投影能量对未知载波相位的不变性，推导相位盲子空间定时误差检测器，并设计前馈捕获度量用于无需载波/偏振恢复的时钟恢复。

Result: 常模设计将0.1% PAPR由6.1 dB降至3.6 dB，低于16-QAM的5.2 dB，但高SNR编码增益约损失1.8 dB；TED在滚降系数低至0.1时仍具有清晰S曲线与稳定锁定点；在块衰落下，估计器SER接近理想定时基准并恢复完整分集，而未校正0.35符号定时偏差会使误码率接近0.4的平台。

Conclusion: 该方案弥合了Grassmannian非相干信号在低PAPR发射与定时恢复方面的实际化缺口，兼具低PAPR、免载波相位恢复和分集接收优势，但目前仍基于符号率块衰落抽象，完整光纤、调制器和相位噪声建模仍需后续研究。

Abstract: Non-coherent Grassmannian (unitary space-time) signaling detects on the received subspace, which is invariant to a branch-side (polarization or mode-coupling) rotation and to a phase that is constant over the coherence block. It therefore needs no carrier-phase or polarization recovery within the block and is robust to phase noise when the per-block phase drift is small, while a multi-branch (polarization or spatial) front end harvests diversity without channel estimation or pilots. However, the Grassmannian-constellation literature usually assumes a distortion-free, linear channel and transmitter and already-acquired symbol timing. This paper closes both gaps while reusing off-the-shelf Grassmannian packings. First, we impose a constant-modulus (low peak-to-average-power-ratio, PAPR) constraint on the constellation and quantify the PAPR/chordal-distance trade-off: a constant-modulus design lowers the 0.1% PAPR from 6.1 dB (unconstrained) to 3.6 dB -- 1.6 dB below 16-QAM (5.2 dB) -- easing the optical modulator linear range and the fiber Kerr-nonlinearity penalty, at a ~1.8 dB cost in high-SNR coding gain. Second, we derive a phase-blind subspace timing-error detector (TED) that exploits the invariance of the GLRT projection energy to the unknown carrier phase, plus a feedforward acquisition metric, supplying clock recovery without prior carrier or polarization recovery. The TED yields a clean S-curve with a stable lock point for roll-offs down to beta=0.1. Under block fading the proposed estimator attains genie-timing SER within a fraction of a dB and recovers full diversity, whereas an uncorrected 0.35-symbol timing offset floors the error rate near 0.4. Results use a symbol-rate block-fading abstraction; full fiber, modulator, and phase-noise modeling is future work. The scheme combines low PAPR with the diversity and phase-recovery-free operation of non-coherent reception.

</details>


### [165] [Measured-Pattern-Aware Pinching-Antenna Systems With Coupling-Efficiency Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26471)
*Hao Feng,Hui Yang,Ming Zeng,Yulei Wang,Ebrahim Bedeer,Nian Xia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种考虑实测辐射方向图、波导衰减与耦合相关功率提取的夹持天线（PA）分析框架，用于更现实地优化单/多PA放置与耦合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PA系统分析常假设各向同性辐射并简化耦合效率，忽略了PA几何结构导致的方向性辐射差异，以及沿波导依次提取导波功率的实际影响，可能导致部署与性能评估不准确。

Method: 构建实测辐射方向图感知的PA框架，将外部测得的方向图、波导传输衰减、以及耦合效率决定的顺序功率提取纳入模型。分别分析单PA放置规则、多PA相位匹配放置，以及均匀耦合和独立可控耦合两类耦合效率设计。

Result: 单PA场景得到平衡方向增益、波导损耗和自由空间路径损耗的放置准则，并推导出优于固定各向同性天线所需的耦合效率阈值。多PA均匀耦合场景得到一维最优性条件，并发现参与相干合并的相位匹配PA数量越多，偏好的耦合效率越低；独立可控耦合场景得到闭式功率分配结构，即有效方向信道更强的PA获得更大辐射功率份额。

Conclusion: 数值结果表明，在PA系统设计中联合考虑实测辐射方向图感知放置与耦合效率优化十分重要，可显著提升模型真实性与系统设计有效性。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna (PA) systems have been widely investigated as a flexible architecture for waveguide-enabled wireless transmission. Existing analytical models, however, often rely on isotropic radiation assumptions and simplified couplingefficiency settings, which may overlook two practical design factors: the geometry-dependent radiation pattern of each PA and the sequential extraction of guided power along the waveguide. In this paper, we propose a measured-radiation-pattern-aware PA framework that incorporates an externally obtained radiation pattern, waveguide attenuation, and coupling-dependent power extraction. For a single PA, the resulting placement rule balances directional gain, waveguide loss, and free-space path loss, leading to a coupling-efficiency threshold for outperforming a fixed isotropic antenna. For multiple PAs, we study phase-matched placement and coupling-efficiency design under both uniform and independently controllable coupling. The uniform-coupling case yields a one-dimensional optimality condition and reveals that the preferred coupling efficiency decreases as more phasematched PAs participate in coherent combining. The independently controllable case admits a closed-form power-allocation structure, where stronger effective directional channels receive larger radiated power fractions. Numerical results based on a representative measured PA radiation pattern demonstrate the importance of jointly accounting for measured-radiation-patternaware placement and coupling-efficiency optimization.

</details>


### [166] [Tight Lower Bounds and Optimal Constructions of Locally Repairable Convertible Codes in the Split Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26742)
*Haoming Shi,Weijun Fang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究系统型最优距离局部可修复码（LRC）之间的高效码转换，完整刻画了在全局拆分场景且 g^I,g^F≤r 参数范围内，稳定最优距离 LRCC 的最优读取带宽成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式存储系统规模、可靠性需求和磁盘失效率会随时间变化，导致纠删码参数需要调整。码转换可将初始编码数据转为最终编码数据，但关键挑战是在保持最优距离和局部修复性质的同时，尽量降低转换过程中的读取带宽。

Method: 论文聚焦系统型最优距离 LRC 的全局拆分转换，在 g^I,g^F≤r 范围内，采用信息论方法推导稳定 LRCC 的读取带宽下界，且不假设初始码、最终码或转换过程具有线性结构。随后基于具有指定修复性质的 MDS 阵列码构造可达下界的方案，并按 g^F=g^I、g^F>g^I、g^F<g^I 三种情形分别设计构造。

Result: 所得下界覆盖整个 g^I,g^F≤r 参数范围；三类构造在各自情形下均达到相应下界，因此证明这些方案在读取带宽意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 论文给出了稳定最优距离局部可修复可转换码在全局拆分场景中的读取带宽最优性刻画，且结论不依赖线性假设，具有较强的一般性和理论完备性。

Abstract: Erasure codes are a key technique for achieving fault-tolerant storage in modern distributed storage systems. As storage systems evolve, their code parameters often need to be adjusted to accommodate changes in storage scale, reliability requirements, and disk failure rates. Such adaptation is performed through code conversion, where data encoded under an initial code are transformed into data encoded under a final code. Convertible codes are designed to carry out this transformation efficiently while preserving desirable properties of the underlying codes.
  In this work, we study conversions between systematic optimal-distance locally repairable codes (LRCs), focusing on the read-bandwidth cost in the global split regime. We concentrate on the parameter range \(g^I,g^F\le r\), where the numbers of initial and final global parity nodes do not exceed the local dimension. For this entire range, we derive read-bandwidth lower bounds for stable optimal-distance locally repairable convertible codes (LRCCs) via an information-theoretic approach, without imposing any linearity assumption on the initial and final codes or on the conversion procedure. We then develop constructions based on MDS array codes with prescribed repair properties. According to the relative sizes of \(g^I\) and \(g^F\), the construction is divided into the three cases \(g^F=g^I\), \(g^F>g^I\), and \(g^F<g^I\); in each case, it attains the corresponding lower bound. Hence we characterize the optimal read-bandwidth cost for stable optimal-distance LRCCs throughout the parameter range \(g^I,g^F\le r\).

</details>


### [167] [Grouped Reverse Importance Sampling for the Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26748)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了反向重要性采样（RIS）的分组变体GRIS，通过对样本成组并使用联合权重来降低分区函数估计的均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通RIS对每个样本单独加权，可能无法充分利用样本组之间的联合结构。研究目标是寻找可计算且能最小化MSE的分组联合权重函数，以提高由Boltzmann分布样本估计分区函数的效率。

Method: 作者将样本划分为大小为k的组，提出非重叠分组GRIS以及基于滑动窗口的FSW和VSW变体；通过归一化MSE与卡方散度之间的恒等式进行理论分析，并研究权重函数的结构性质，尤其是依赖总能量的group-energy权重函数。

Result: 理论上证明，任何优于普通RIS的权重必须耦合组内成分，乘积形式权重会恶化MSE；同时对于非重叠分组，无损最优性地只需考虑依赖组内总能量的权重函数。数值上，k=2和k=3时，非重叠分组MSE在三个例子中降低约20%--65%；FSW优于NOL，VSW进一步提升。

Conclusion: GRIS通过组内联合加权显著改进RIS的分区函数估计效率；理论结果大幅简化了最优权重搜索空间，而滑动窗口变体在实践中具有更强性能，但其相应最优性理论仍存在开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze several grouped variants of the method of reverse importance sampling (RIS) for estimating a partition function from samples of the Boltzmann distribution $p(x)=e^{ \betaU(x)}/Z(β)$. Ordinary RIS weighs each sample separately. By contrast, our proposed grouped RIS (GRIS) methods are based on assigning the samples into groups (or batches) of size $k\ge 2$ and applying a joint weight function to each group. The focal point of the research is the quest for a tractable weight function that would yield the smallest possible mean squared error (MSE). A simple identity relates the normalized MSE to the chi-squared divergence between the joint-weight distribution and the distribution of the $k$-fold sum of independent energies. Our first theoretical finding is that any weight that improves on ordinary RIS ($k=1$) must couple the group components. In other words, it must not be a product-form function across those components, as product-form weight functions always worsen the MSE. Our second, and more important, finding is that, without loss of optimality, it is sufficient to seek weight functions that depend only on the total energy, $\sum_iU(x_i)$, of the group (group-energy weight functions); for the sliding-window variants, the analogous result is open. This finding simplifies both the theoretical analysis and the application of the method substantially. For $k=2$ and $k=3$, the MSE associated with non-overlapping (NOL) groups is reduced by $20$--$65\%$ across three examples. We then propose two additional variants of GRIS, both based on sliding-window grouping (as opposed to NOL grouping). The first applies a fixed weight sliding window (FSW) across all (cyclic) shifts of the sliding window, and the second allows a variable-weight sliding window (VSW). The FSW scheme improves on the NOL one, and the VSW improves even further, as will be demonstrated numerically.

</details>


### [168] [An Orthogonal Approximate Message Passing Framework for Multiuser Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26777)
*Burak Çakmak,Hao Yan,Alexander Fengler,Giuseppe Caire,Lei Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于线性高斯多用户通信系统的 Bayes 最优迭代信号恢复框架，适用于随机右酉不变预编码和非可分编码信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OAMP/VAMP 等迭代推断方法主要针对较简单或可分先验模型；对于带随机预编码、多用户、一般线性信道以及非可分编码信号的通信系统，如何构造 Bayes 最优迭代恢复算法仍是开放问题。

Method: 作者建立接收模型 y=Σu Hu Ξu su+n，提出一种新的 OAMP/VAMP 类型框架，可视为期望传播 EP 与 OAMP 的插值；并给出高维有限样本分析。同时，利用新的 disorder-averaging 技术，在 replica-symmetric 假设下分析对应推断问题。

Result: 严格的高维算法分析与 RS ansatz 推导得到一致的解耦原理，表明多用户复杂系统在渐近意义下可分解为等效的低维/标量推断问题；在 RS 假设成立时，所提算法达到渐近 Bayes 最优。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了随机预编码线性高斯多用户系统中 Bayes 最优迭代恢复算法的构造问题，并在理论上连接了 EP、OAMP/VAMP、有限样本高维分析与 replica 方法。

Abstract: We solve the open problem of constructing a Bayes-optimal iterative signal recovery algorithm for linear-Gaussian \emph{multiuser} communication systems with random precoding at the transmitters. Specifically, we consider the received signal model $\mathbf{y} = \sum_{u} \mathbf{H}_u \mathbfΞ_u \mathbf{s}_u + \mathbf{n}$, where $\mathbf{n}$ is white Gaussian noise, $\{\mathbf{H}_u \in \mathbb{C}^{L \times L}\}$ are discrete-time channel matrices -- modeling a wide class of generally time-varying and dispersive linear channels with possibly multiple antennas -- and the precoding matrices $\{\boldsymbolΞ_u \in \mathbb{C}^{L \times N_u}\}$ are drawn independently from a right-unitarily invariant random matrix ensemble. We consider generic \emph{non-separable} (coded) systems where the users' signals $\{\mathbf{s}_u\}$ follow general (non-factorizing) distributions. For this model, we introduce a novel orthogonal/vector approximate message passing (OAMP/VAMP)-type framework, including an algorithm and its high-dimensional (but finite-sample) analysis. From an algorithmic standpoint, the proposed method can be interpreted as an \emph{interpolation} between Minka's expectation propagation (EP)--a widely used method in machine learning--and OAMP. Our main theoretical contribution is the explicit finite-sample analysis of the proposed algorithm. Furthermore, we analyze the associated inference problem via a replica-symmetric (RS) ansatz by using a novel disorder-averaging technique. Both the (rigorous) high-dimensional analysis of the algorithm and the RS ansatz reveal the same decoupling principle, establishing that the proposed algorithm is asymptotically Bayes-optimal under the validity of the RS ansatz.

</details>


### [169] [An Information-Theoretic Metric for Semantic Value of Spatiotemporal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26844)
*Zijing Wang,Zhijin Qin,Siyu Lin,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于互信息的语义信息价值（SVoI）度量，用于量化时空相关语义信息对未知系统状态预测不确定性的降低程度，并在高斯马尔可夫模型下给出闭式表达与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络规模和数据量快速增长导致频谱资源受限；语义通信通过传输面向任务的重要语义信息缓解带宽瓶颈。但在实时无线应用中，信息语义受源内在动态和环境外在动态影响，呈现复杂时空相关性，因此需要可量化其任务价值的信息论指标。

Method: 提出基于互信息的Semantic Value of Information（SVoI）框架，将过去具有时空相关性的语义观测用于预测未知系统状态时带来的不确定性降低作为价值度量。针对一般高斯马尔可夫模型推导SVoI闭式表达，并解析研究可分离与耦合时空相关性对SVoI的影响，同时考虑信息时效性和信道条件。

Result: 论文获得了高斯马尔可夫情形下SVoI及其界的闭式/解析结果，并通过数值仿真验证理论分析。结果表明SVoI能够刻画源语义时空相关性、信息新鲜度以及信道条件对语义价值的联合影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的SVoI指标为语义感知通信系统提供了有效的优化目标，可用于下一代无线语义通信中的资源分配、信息调度和任务导向传输设计。

Abstract: With the explosive growth of network scale and data volume, wireless communication is facing an increasingly severe limitation of spectrum resources. Semantic communication has emerged as a promising paradigm to break the bandwidth bottleneck by transmitting significant task-oriented semantic information rather than raw data. In practical real-time wireless applications, semantics of information exhibit diverse spatial and temporal correlations depending on intrinsic dynamics of source and extrinsic dynamics of environment. Motivated by this observation, this paper develops a novel information-theoretic metric to quantify the semantic value of spatiotemporal information. Specifically, a semantic value of information (SVoI) framework is proposed based on the mutual information, which characterises the reduction in uncertainty when predicting an unknown system state using past semantic spatiotemporal correlated observations. Focusing on general Gaussian Markov models, closed-form expressions of the SVoI are derived. Effects of both separable and coupled spatiotemporal correlations on SVoI are further investigated analytically. Numerical simulations are conducted to validate the theoretical analysis of SVoI and its bounds. The proposed SVoI metric jointly captures the impact of semantic spatiotemporal correlation of source, timeliness of information, and channel conditions, which could serve as an effective optimisation objective for the design of next-generation semantic-aware communication systems.

</details>


### [170] [A $q$-ary Local Criterion for the Radius-One Limited Permutation Channel and Almost-Optimal Binary Block-Concatenation Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26905)
*Noam Ben Shimon,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究半径一有限置换信道 LPC∞(1) 的零错误块拼接码，提出局部判据、证明其速率完备性，并给出检测码构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局零错误混淆性判定通常涉及所有长度的码字组合，复杂且难以用于构造高率码；作者希望用有限的局部块间检查替代全局问题，并进一步理解纠错与检错场景下的可达速率。

Method: 提出 q 元两阶段局部充分判据：同长度块使用截断球测试，非等长块使用第二阶段前缀测试；证明该判据在速率意义上完备；设计精确的乘积自动机验证器，用于固定二元前缀自由块集的全长度纠错判定；同时建立 q 元检错配对上界与局部检错判据。

Result: 在二元情形构造出速率为 0.649872、0.652018、0.653618 的纠错块拼接码，优于先前 0.642805 的字符串拼接速率，距离已知上界 2/3 仅 0.013049；检错方面构造出二元速率 0.756707 的块拼接码，上界为 1/2 log2 3≈0.792481。

Conclusion: 局部判据虽为充分条件，但在渐近速率上不损失能力，其认证的 q 元块率上确界等于 q 元零错误容量；块拼接方法能有效提升 LPC∞(1) 信道下的零错误纠错与检错码率。

Abstract: The radius-one limited permutation channel $\operatorname{LPC}_{\infty}(1)$ maps a transmitted word to any word obtained by an arbitrary set of pairwise disjoint adjacent transpositions. This is the $r=1$ case of the $\ell_\infty$-limited permutation channel of Langberg et al., and is also the zero-error version of simultaneous adjacent-swap errors. We study zero-error block-concatenation codes for this channel.
  Our first contribution is a $q$-ary two-stage local criterion for certifying free block-concatenation codes. The criterion replaces the global all-length confusability problem by finitely many local checks between blocks: a same-length truncated-ball test and a second-stage prefix test for unequal lengths. In the binary case, it yields explicit block-concatenation codes of rates $0.649872$, $0.652018$, and $0.653618$. The best construction improves the previous string-concatenation rate $0.642805$ and comes within $0.013049$ of the known upper bound $2/3$.
  Although the criterion is only sufficient, we prove that it is rate-complete: for every alphabet size $q$, the supremum of $q$-ary block rates certified by the criterion is exactly the $q$-ary zero-error capacity $C_0^{(q)}$. Thus it imposes no asymptotic rate loss. We also give an exact product-automaton verifier which decides, for a fixed prefix-free binary block set, whether the induced finite-length codes are correcting for all lengths.
  Finally, motivated by feedback settings, we study error detection. We prove a $q$-ary pairing upper bound and give a $q$-ary local detecting criterion. In the binary case, we construct a detecting block-concatenation code of rate $0.756707$, compared with the upper bound $\frac12\log_2 3\approx0.792481$.

</details>


### [171] [Mismatched Exponents for Deterministic and Randomised Noise-Guessing Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26954)
*Henrique K. Miyamoto,Richard Combes,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文分析加性无记忆信道中确定性与随机化噪声猜测译码在失配度量下的错误指数与复杂度指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声猜测译码的性能不仅取决于信道和码率，也取决于译码度量；已有匹配度量未必在随机化场景下同时达到最优错误与复杂度指数，因此需要系统研究不同度量的指数性能与普适方案。

Method: 作者建立失配译码度量下确定性和随机化噪声猜测译码的错误指数与复杂度指数分析框架，并分别考察匹配度量、α-倾斜度量以及基于噪声序列经验熵的普适度量。

Result: 在确定性译码中，α-倾斜不改变性能，所有α>0的倾斜度量均等价于匹配度量且在平均错误和复杂度上最优；在随机化译码中，匹配度量对复杂度指数并非最优，需要随码率调节α才能同时实现最优指数；经验熵普适度量可在未知信道规律且所有码率下同时达到最优错误与复杂度指数。

Conclusion: 确定性噪声猜测译码对α-倾斜具有鲁棒性，而随机化版本对度量选择更敏感；基于经验熵的普适译码度量提供了无需信道先验、统一适用于不同码率并同时最优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study both the deterministic and randomised variants of noise-guessing decoding in additive memoryless channels. The error and complexity exponents of such decoding schemes are analysed under mismatched decoding metrics, and then specialised to matched, $α$-tilted, and universal decoding metrics. The $α$-tilted metric is proportional to the $α$-th power ($α>0$) of the true noise distribution. In deterministic decoding, the tilting operation does not affect the performance: all these metrics are equivalent to the matched one ($α=1$), and are optimal for both average error and complexity. On the other hand, in randomised decoding, the matched metric is not optimal for complexity exponents; we show that the decoder needs to tune the parameter~$α$ according to the code rate in order to simultaneously achieve both optimal exponents using a decoding metric in that family. Finally, a universal decoding metric based on the empirical entropy of the noise sequence achieves both optimal exponents, independently of the channel law and uniformly over code rates, for the deterministic and randomised variants.

</details>


### [172] [Algorithms for Threshold Group Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27127)
*Amin Coja-Oghlan,Remco van der Hofstad,Lena Krieg,Noela Müller,Connor Riddlesden,Olga Scheftelowitsch*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无间隙、无噪声、非自适应阈值群测试问题，提出一种基于空间耦合测试设计的高效推断算法，可在常列设计所需的最小测试数下以高概率精确恢复稀疏二元向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阈值群测试在常列测试设计下已知存在尖锐的信息论相变，但缺少能够以高效计算方式达到该信息论阈值的算法；同时相关二元群测试算法分析复杂，依赖繁琐的加权和技术。

Method: 作者构造空间耦合的非自适应测试设计，并设计相应的高效推断算法，用于从阈值化的池化测试结果中精确识别稀疏缺陷项；分析上避免复杂加权和，采用更直接的证明框架。

Result: 该算法在常列设计所需的最小非自适应测试数量下，能够以高概率实现精确恢复，匹配自然基准测试设计的信息论阈值，并提供接近最优的性能保证。

Conclusion: 空间耦合设计可使无间隙阈值群测试在计算效率与样本效率上同时达到优良表现，证明技术较以往相关群测试算法更简洁，并具有信息论最优意义上的竞争力。

Abstract: We study the Threshold Group Testing (TGT) problem without a gap in the noiseless, non-adaptive setting, where the goal is to exactly recover a sparse binary vector from pooled test outcomes using as few tests as possible. In TGT, a test applied to a subset of items returns a positive outcome if the number of defective items in the subset reaches a prescribed threshold, and a negative outcome otherwise. Under the assumption of an analytic condition, TGT has been shown to undergo a sharp information-theoretic phase transition for exact recovery on the class of constant-column test designs. In this paper, we develop an efficient inference algorithm that achieves exact recovery with high probability using the minimum number of non-adaptive tests that are needed for the constant-column design, thereby matching the information-theoretic threshold of a natural benchmark test design. Our approach is based on a spatially coupled test design and admits a significantly simpler analysis than existing algorithms for related group testing problems. In particular, unlike previous methods for binary group testing, our algorithm does not rely on the analysis of intricate weighted sums. This leads to a more straightforward proof technique, while still allowing near-optimal performance guarantees.

</details>


### [173] [Linear Code Conversion in the Merge Regime: General Bounds and Reed--Muller Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27179)
*Anina Gruica,Benjamin Jany,Stanislav Kruglik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意标量线性码在合并场景下的码转换问题，给出通用读写代价下界，并用广义汉明重量细化分析；进一步构造 Reed-Muller 可转换码，在特定参数下达到写代价下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式存储系统中，擦除码需随节点失效率变化调整参数以节省存储。直接重编码资源开销大，而现有码转换研究多限于特定码类，缺乏适用于任意线性码的统一理论。

Method: 作者在合并场景下建立标量线性码转换框架，从未改变符号和读取符号角度推导通用写、读代价下界；引入广义汉明重量刻画子码支撑集增长，以获得比仅基于最小距离更精细的下界；并基于 Plotkin 分解构造 Reed-Muller 可转换码。

Result: 该框架可恢复若干重要特殊情形中的已知界；当最终码的广义汉明重量层级存在非平凡跳跃时，新界严格更强。对 Reed-Muller 码，在自然参数区间内构造达到写代价下界；读代价方面，对一个初始块的广义汉明重量分析是紧的，另一个块仍存在间隙。

Conclusion: 论文提供了任意线性码合并转换的统一下界理论，并展示广义汉明重量在刻画码转换代价中的优势；Reed-Muller 构造验证了理论的有效性，但读代价最优性仍有待进一步解决。

Abstract: Erasure codes are a core component of most existing large-scale distributed storage systems, ensuring reliability against node failures. Recent work has shown that adapting code parameters to changing node failure rates can lead to significant storage savings. The default approach is to re-encode the data under a new code, which consumes substantial system resources. Code conversion was introduced to reduce this cost. However, existing work has mainly focused on conversions within specific classes of codes. In this paper, we study scalar linear code conversion in the merge regime for arbitrary linear codes. We derive universal lower bounds on the write and read costs in terms of unchanged and read symbols. The bounds are refined using generalized Hamming weights, which capture support-growth properties of subcodes and can give sharper estimates than minimum-distance-only arguments. We show that the framework recovers known bounds for important special cases and can be strictly stronger when the final code has nontrivial jumps in its generalized Hamming weight hierarchy. We then apply the framework to Reed-Muller codes and construct explicit Reed-Muller convertible codes using the Plotkin decomposition. For a natural Reed-Muller parameter regime, the construction attains the derived write-cost lower bound. For the read cost, the generalized-Hamming-weight analysis is sharp for one initial block, while a gap remains for the other block.

</details>


### [174] [All you need is log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27349)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了多分布场景下 Rényi 散度的规范推广：所有满足数据处理单调性与独立乘积可加性的 W 元分布泛函，均可表示为多路 coincidence divergences 的正积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Rényi 散度完整解决了两个概率分布之间比较的问题，但多总体公平、多先验 PAC-Bayes、多假设检验等任务需要同时比较多个分布。什么是多分布情形下对应的“正确”Rényi 型散度族仍是开放问题。

Method: 论文从结构公理出发，研究对 W 个分布的泛函，要求其满足数据处理不增性和独立乘积可加性，并证明其可由多路 coincidence divergence C_α 的正积分表示。参数空间被分解为四类层：单纯形内部、混合符号指数锥、无穷远 tropical 边界以及单纯形顶点处的成对 KL 边。论文还通过 Kolmogorov-Nagumo 均值、经典熵刻画、多假设检验误差指数和多彩票投注解释等独立路线验证该族的自然性。

Result: 得到完整表征定理：每个满足公理的多分布散度都是 C_α(π_1,...,π_W)=-log∫π_1^{α_1}...π_W^{α_W} 的正积分，其中参数满足相应分层结构。四个层级均不可省略，因为每一层都对应其他层无法表示的显式散度，并且都可视为单纯形内部原子的极限。两分布情形退化为标准 Rényi 结果，并给出 W=3 示例、数值验证与条件扩展。

Conclusion: 论文提出并论证了一个统一的多分布 Rényi 微积分框架，认为多路 coincidence divergences 及其边界极限构成多分布比较问题中的规范散度族，而非某一组公理下的偶然产物。

Abstract: Comparing two probability distributions is a basic building block of statistics and machine learning, and the right family is well understood: the Rényi divergences of order $α\in[0,\infty]$ are the unique family monotone under data processing and additive on independent products. Many problems instead compare more than two distributions at once -- multi-population fairness, multi-prior PAC-Bayes bounds, multi-hypothesis testing -- and the right multi-distribution generalization of the Rényi family has been an open question.
  We characterize it. Every functional of $W$-tuples of distributions that is monotone under data processing and additive on independent products is a positive integral of multi-way coincidence divergences $C_α(π_1,\dots,π_W) := -\log\int π_1^{α_1}\cdotsπ_W^{α_W}$ (with $\sum_k α_k = 1$) over a parameter space with four strata: the simplex interior; mixed-sign exponent cones (the analogue of Rényi orders $>1$); a tropical boundary at infinity carrying max-divergences; and pairwise Kullback-Leibler edges at the simplex vertices. Each stratum is necessary -- the destination of an explicit data-processing-monotone, product-additive divergence the others cannot reproduce -- and each is a clean limit of simplex-interior atoms.
  The same family arises from five independent routes -- the structural axioms, Kolmogorov-Nagumo means with Rényi's entropy axiomatics, classical entropy characterizations, multi-hypothesis testing error exponents, and a multi-lottery betting interpretation -- structural evidence that this is the canonical multi-distribution Rényi calculus rather than an artefact of any one axiomatic input. The two-prior case recovers the standard Rényi result; a worked $W=3$ instance, numerical verification, and a conditional extension round out the treatment.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代式数据生成流程，通过构造呈线性梯度变化的级联样本来发现与模型行为相关的激活特征，并用于检测与控制语言模型的谄媚倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活转向方法依赖大量二元对比样本，但这类样本往往难以清晰分离导致特定行为的模型特征，从而限制了可解释性框架识别行为因果特征以及稳定控制模型行为的能力。

Method: 作者提出超越简单二元样本对的迭代数据生成管线，构造在目标行为强度上呈线性变化的级联样本，用以隔离与行为线性相关的特征；并在语言模型谄媚行为检测与转向任务中分析这些特征形成的子空间及其可分性。

Result: 实验显示，通过级联样本发现的谄媚相关特征形成线性可分子空间，能够选择出更清晰对应目标行为的模型激活；在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒转向方面，相比 LLM-as-a-judge 与系统提示基线达到相当或更好的效果，同时计算需求更低。

Conclusion: 级联线性特征样本能够提升行为相关特征的解耦与可解释性，使激活转向更可靠、更高效，并为语言模型行为检测和控制提供更具解释保证的方法。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [176] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，当基准准确率趋于饱和时，不应简单淘汰基准，而应继续从构念效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架贡献、人机协作增益等维度评估智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测范式过度关注准确率；一旦基准准确率饱和，通常会被更难版本替代。这会忽略智能体性能中同样关键但未被充分测量的维度，导致对能力、鲁棒性和实际应用价值的理解不完整。

Method: 作者以用于科学代码计算可复现性的 CORE-Bench Hard 为案例，分析其在高性能智能体下暴露出的构念效度问题；提出改进版 CORE-Bench v1.1 和分布外任务套件 CORE-Bench OOD；并在真实计算可复现任务上开展小规模随机实验，评估人机协作带来的效率提升。

Result: 研究发现，即使准确率已经饱和，CORE-Bench v1.1 仍可有效衡量效率、可靠性、模型性能和脚手架性能；同时，人机协作在真实任务中带来约 2 倍的显著加速，且该估计可能因部分人工组任务超时而偏低。

Conclusion: 论文主张用多维度评估替代以准确率为中心的基准淘汰范式，证明饱和基准仍能为理解智能体性能提供重要信息，并为更严谨的智能体评测提供了方法论方向。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [177] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调聊天模型中的“顺从人格”方向会门控拒答行为，说明拒答并非孤立的线性方向机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分别识别了激活空间中与拒答和人格特质相关的线性方向，但通常将二者视为独立机制。本文旨在探究顺从型 persona 是否会影响或调控模型的拒答表达。

Method: 作者在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 中分别提取顺从模型人格方向与拒答方向，并对两类方向进行激活干预、重新注入拒答方向、以及在特定层窗口中投影移除 persona 方向，并与随机方向投影作对照。

Result: 顺从 persona steering 显著抑制拒答；在 Llama 中拒答率从 97% 降至 2%。在较晚层重新引入拒答方向可部分恢复拒答，而在早期层无效。移除晚层窗口中的 persona 方向可将拒答恢复至基线，随机方向则不能。

Conclusion: 拒答机制受到顺从人格方向的门控，且该门控发生在较晚层的表达阶段，位于拒答计算之后。因此，将拒答视为单一、孤立的线性方向是不充分的。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [178] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlgoEvolve：一种由大语言模型驱动的进化式程序合成框架，用于生成、评估并迭代改进可执行的算法交易策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 作为语义变异算子的研究多集中于静态编程基准；而算法交易环境具有噪声高、非平稳、收益/行为不连续等特点，更能检验持续程序发现与自适应策略生成能力。

Method: 框架将交易策略表示为 Python 代码，由 LLM 生成和变异，并通过严格测试协议评估；同时引入元进化外循环，对内循环中用于程序合成的提示词进行演化，以自动发现更优搜索启发式。

Result: 实验显示系统能涌现出制度/市场状态自适应的策略逻辑，包括自主切换交易规则；元进化提示词能更好平衡探索与利用，减少零交易失败，并持续优于初始人工设计指令。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的语义进化可作为复杂、动态环境中持续程序合成的可行方法，尤其适用于需要适应非平稳条件的策略发现任务。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [179] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由大语言模型驱动的比较分析流程，用于大规模研究AI智能体互操作标准的治理话语，并比较ERC-8004与Google A2A两种治理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体协议快速增加，其互操作标准背后的治理结构如何影响参与、议题设置与权力分布仍缺乏实证研究。作者希望用可扩展的计算方法填补技术治理研究中的经验空白。

Method: 研究构建了结合LLM辅助标注、神经主题建模与多层网络分析的治理话语分析管线，并应用于ERC-8004和Google A2A两个对比鲜明的智能体互操作标准；数据包含4,323条治理参与记录。

Result: 研究发现，治理形式会影响实质性议题重点；但两种机制都表现出相近的参与不平等与社群碎片化水平。无许可、链上治理环境中的话语对齐更密集，说明开放治理可能在去中心化参与下促进更强的主题收敛。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，LLM辅助方法可用于推进技术治理的实证分析，并为设计更公平的智能体AI标准提供启示；其数据与代码公开，有利于复现与扩展研究。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [180] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保留来源出处的知识图谱多智能体框架，整合患者社区叙事与监管不良事件数据，用于更可审计地组织抗抑郁药安全信息。


<details>
